- **agent-memory-hub**: Recall or record cross-agent work history from the shared Agent Memory Hub. Use when the user refers to something done in a PREVIOUS session or another coding tool ("what did I do last time…", "we solved this before", "the Netlify fix from before", "그때 그거", "지난번에"), asks to search past work, or wants to refresh/collect the shared memory. The hub aggregates read-only session history from Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Copilot, Antigravity and Gemini. - **agent-memory-hygiene**: Use when deciding when and how to commit items to a project memory system. Covers session boundary definitions, item quality rules, and the tradeoff between over-committing (noise) and under-committing (lost learnings). ([georghildebrand/usefull_agent_skills](https://github.com/georghildebrand/usefull_agent_skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-memory-hygiene) · ★1)
- **agent-memory-hygiene**: Keep an agent's persistent memory trustworthy — every entry has provenance, entries expire and get re-verified, secrets never land in memory, and untrusted content is never stored as fact — so recall stays reliable instead of slowly poisoning the agent. Use this when agents write to and read from a long-lived memory store, when old remembered "facts" start misleading current runs, when memory grows without bound, or when you worry a prompt injection could persist into memory. The recall a stale or poisoned memory returns is worse than no memory at all. ([sharp-skills/skills](https://github.com/sharp-skills/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-memory-hygiene) · ★1)
